       get(&quot;/store&quot;);
then().
       body(&quot;store.book.author*.length().sum()&quot;, greaterThan(50))."><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/store"</span>);
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.book.author*.length().sum()"</span>, <span class="pl-en">greaterThan</span>(<span class="pl-c1">50</span>)).</pre></div>
<p>And of course we can use <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-path/6.0.1/io/restassured/path/json/JsonPath.html" rel="nofollow">JsonPath</a> to actually return the result:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Get the response body as a string
String response = get(&quot;/store&quot;).asString();
// Get the sum of all author length's as an int. &quot;from&quot; is again statically imported from the JsonPath class
int sumOfAllAuthorLengths = from(response).getInt(&quot;store.book.author*.length().sum()&quot;);
// We can also assert that the sum is equal to 53 as expected.
assertThat(sumOfAllAuthorLengths, is(53));"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Get the response body as a string</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/store"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asString</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// Get the sum of all author length's as an int. "from" is again statically imported from the JsonPath class</span>
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">sumOfAllAuthorLengths</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">response</span>).<span class="pl-en">getInt</span>(<span class="pl-s">"store.book.author*.length().sum()"</span>);
<span class="pl-c">// We can also assert that the sum is equal to 53 as expected.</span>
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">sumOfAllAuthorLengths</span>, <span class="pl-en">is</span>(<span class="pl-c1">53</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Deserialization with Generics</h2><a id="user-content-deserialization-with-generics" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deserialization with Generics" href="#deserialization-with-generics"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>REST Assured 3.3.0 introduced the <code>io.restassured.mapper.TypeRef</code> class that allows you to de-serialize the response to a container with a generic type. For example let's say that you have a service that returns the following JSON for a GET request to <code>/products</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="[
          {
              &quot;id&quot;: 2,
              &quot;name&quot;: &quot;An ice sculpture&quot;,
              &quot;price&quot;: 12.50,
              &quot;tags&quot;: [&quot;cold&quot;, &quot;ice&quot;],
              &quot;dimensions&quot;: {
                  &quot;length&quot;: 7.0,
                  &quot;width&quot;: 12.0,
                  &quot;height&quot;: 9.5
              },
              &quot;warehouseLocation&quot;: {
                  &quot;latitude&quot;: -78.75,
                  &quot;longitude&quot;: 20.4
              }
          },
          {
              &quot;id&quot;: 3,
              &quot;name&quot;: &quot;A blue mouse&quot;,
              &quot;price&quot;: 25.50,
                  &quot;dimensions&quot;: {
                  &quot;length&quot;: 3.1,
                  &quot;width&quot;: 1.0,
                  &quot;height&quot;: 1.0
              },
              &quot;warehouseLocation&quot;: {
                  &quot;latitude&quot;: 54.4,
                  &quot;longitude&quot;: -32.7
              }
          }
      ]"><pre>[
          {
              <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">2</span>,
              <span class="pl-ent">"name"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>An ice sculpture<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
              <span class="pl-ent">"price"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">12.50</span>,
              <span class="pl-ent">"tags"</span>: [<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>cold<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>, <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ice<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>],
              <span class="pl-ent">"dimensions"</span>: {
                  <span class="pl-ent">"length"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">7.0</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"width"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">12.0</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"height"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">9.5</span>
              },
              <span class="pl-ent">"warehouseLocation"</span>: {
                  <span class="pl-ent">"latitude"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">-78.75</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"longitude"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">20.4</span>
              }
          },
          {
              <span class="pl-ent">"id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3</span>,
              <span class="pl-ent">"name"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>A blue mouse<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
              <span class="pl-ent">"price"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">25.50</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"dimensions"</span>: {
                  <span class="pl-ent">"length"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">3.1</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"width"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1.0</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"height"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">1.0</span>
              },
              <span class="pl-ent">"warehouseLocation"</span>: {
                  <span class="pl-ent">"latitude"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">54.4</span>,
                  <span class="pl-ent">"longitude"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">-32.7</span>
              }
          }
      ]</pre></div>
<p>You can then extract the root list to a <code>List&lt;Map&lt;String, Object&gt;&gt;</code> (or a any generic container of choice) using the <code>TypeRef</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="// Extract
List&lt;Map&lt;String, Object&gt;&gt; products = get(&quot;/products&quot;).as(new TypeRef&lt;List&lt;Map&lt;String, Object&gt;&gt;&gt;() {});

// Now you can do validations on the extracted objects:
assertThat(products, hasSize(2));
assertThat(products.get(0).get(&quot;id&quot;), equalTo(2));
assertThat(products.get(0).get(&quot;name&quot;), equalTo(&quot;An ice sculpture&quot;));
assertThat(products.get(0).get(&quot;price&quot;), equalTo(12.5));
assertThat(products.get(1).get(&quot;id&quot;), equalTo(3));
assertThat(products.get(1).get(&quot;name&quot;), equalTo(&quot;A blue mouse&quot;));
assertThat(products.get(1).get(&quot;price&quot;), equalTo(25.5));```"><pre><span class="pl-c">// Extract</span>
<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">Map</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Object</span>&gt;&gt; <span class="pl-s1">products</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/products"</span>).<span class="pl-en">as</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">TypeRef</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">Map</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Object</span>&gt;&gt;&gt;() {});

<span class="pl-c">// Now you can do validations on the extracted objects:</span>
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>, <span class="pl-en">hasSize</span>(<span class="pl-c1">2</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"id"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">2</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"name"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"An ice sculpture"</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">0</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12.5</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"id"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">3</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"name"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"A blue mouse"</span>));
<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>(<span class="pl-s1">products</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>), <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">25.5</span>));```</pre></div>
<p>Note that currently this only works for JSON responses.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Additional Examples</h2><a id="user-content-additional-examples" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Additional Examples" href="#additional-examples"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Micha Kops has written a really good blog with several examples (including code examples that you can checkout). You can read it <a href="https://www.hascode.com/testing-restful-web-services-made-easy-using-the-rest-assured-framework/" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<p>Also <a href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/basdijkstra" rel="nofollow">Bas Dijkstra</a> has been generous enough to open source his REST Assured workshop. You can read more about this <a href="http://www.ontestautomation.com/open-sourcing-my-workshop-an-experiment/" rel="nofollow">here</a> and you can try out, and contribute to, the exercises available in <a href="https://github.com/basdijkstra/workshops/">his</a> github repository.</p>
<p>Bas has also made a nice introductory screencast to REST Assured, you can find it <a href="https://testautomationu.applitools.com/automating-your-api-tests-with-rest-assured/" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Note on floats and doubles</h2><a id="user-content-note-on-floats-and-doubles" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Note on floats and doubles" href="#note-on-floats-and-doubles"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Floating point numbers must be compared with a Java "float" primitive. For example, if we consider the following JSON object:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{

    &quot;price&quot;:12.12 

}"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span>

    <span class="pl-s">"price"</span>:<span class="pl-c1">12.12</span> 

<span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>the following test will fail, because we compare with a "double" instead of a "float":</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/price&quot;).then().assertThat().body(&quot;price&quot;, equalTo(12.12));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/price"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12.12</span>));</pre></div>
<p>Instead, compare with a float with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/price&quot;).then().assertThat().body(&quot;price&quot;, equalTo(12.12f));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/price"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"price"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">12.12f</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Note on syntax</h2><a id="user-content-note-on-syntax" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Note on syntax" href="#note-on-syntax"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>When reading blogs about REST Assured you may see a lot of examples using the "given / expect / when" syntax, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;).
expect().
        body(&quot;lotto.lottoId&quot;, equalTo(5)).
when().
        get(&quot;/lotto&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">expect</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoId"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">5</span>)).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>This is the so called "legacy syntax" which was the de facto way of writing tests in REST Assured 1.x. While this works fine it turned out to be quite confusing and annoying for many users. The reason for not using "given / when / then" in the first place was mainly technical. So prior to REST Assured 2.0 there was no support "given / when / then" which is more or less the standard approach when you're doing some kind of BDD-like testing. The "given / expect / when" approach still works fine in 2.0 and above but "given / when / then" reads better and is easier to understand for most people and is thus recommended in most cases. There's however one benefit of using the "given / expect / when" approach and that is that ALL expectation errors can be displayed at the same time which is not possible with the new syntax (since the expectations are defined last). This means that if you would have had multiple expectations in the previous example such as</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;).
expect().
        statusCode(400).
        body(&quot;lotto.lottoId&quot;, equalTo(6)).
when().
        get(&quot;/lotto&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">expect</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">400</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoId"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">6</span>)).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will report that both the status code expectation and the body expectation are wrong. Rewriting this with the new syntax</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(400).
        body(&quot;lotto.lottoId&quot;, equalTo(6));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">400</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoId"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-c1">6</span>));</pre></div>
<p>will only report an error at the first failed expectation / assertion (that status code was expected to be 400 but it was actually 200). You would have to re-run the test in order to catch the second error.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Syntactic Sugar</h3><a id="user-content-syntactic-sugar" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Syntactic Sugar" href="#syntactic-sugar"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Another thing worth mentioning is that REST Assured contains some methods that are only there for syntactic sugar. For example the "and" method which can add readability if you're writing everything in a one-liner, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;).and().header(&quot;z&quot;, &quot;w&quot;).when().get(&quot;/something&quot;).then().assertThat().statusCode(200).and().body(&quot;x.y&quot;, equalTo(&quot;z&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"z"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"w"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"z"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>This is the same thing as:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        param(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;y&quot;).
        header(&quot;z&quot;, &quot;w&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/something&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200).
        body(&quot;x.y&quot;, equalTo(&quot;z&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"y"</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"z"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"w"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x.y"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"z"</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Getting Response Data</h1><a id="user-content-getting-response-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Getting Response Data" href="#getting-response-data"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also get the content of a response. E.g. let's say you want to return the body of a get request to "/lotto". You can get it a variety of different ways:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="InputStream stream = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).asInputStream(); // Don't forget to close this one when you're done
byte[] byteArray = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).asByteArray();
String json = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).asString();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">InputStream</span> <span class="pl-s1">stream</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asInputStream</span>(); <span class="pl-c">// Don't forget to close this one when you're done</span>
<span class="pl-smi">byte</span>[] <span class="pl-s1">byteArray</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asByteArray</span>();
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">json</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">asString</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Extracting values from the Response after validation</h2><a id="user-content-extracting-values-from-the-response-after-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Extracting values from the Response after validation" href="#extracting-values-from-the-response-after-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can extract values from the response or return the response instance itself after you've done validating the response by using the <code>extract</code> method. This is useful for example if you want to use values from the response in sequent requests. For example given that a resource called <code>title</code> returns the following JSON</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=" {
     &quot;title&quot; : &quot;My Title&quot;,
      &quot;_links&quot;: {
              &quot;self&quot;: { &quot;href&quot;: &quot;/title&quot; },
              &quot;next&quot;: { &quot;href&quot;: &quot;/title?page=2&quot; }
           }
 }"><pre> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
     <span class="pl-s">"title"</span> : <span class="pl-s">"My Title"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
      <span class="pl-s">"_links"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
              <span class="pl-s">"self"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s">"href"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"/title"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span>
              <span class="pl-s">"next"</span>: <span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s">"href"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"/title?page=2"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
           <span class="pl-kos">}</span>
 <span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>and you want to validate that content type is equal to <code>JSON</code> and the title is equal to <code>My Title</code>
but you also want to extract the link to the <code>next</code> title to use that in a subsequent request. This is how:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String nextTitleLink =
given().
        param(&quot;param_name&quot;, &quot;param_value&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/title&quot;).
then().
        contentType(JSON).
        body(&quot;title&quot;, equalTo(&quot;My Title&quot;)).
extract().
        path(&quot;_links.next.href&quot;);

get(nextTitleLink). .."><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">nextTitleLink</span> =
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param_name"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"param_value"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/title"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">contentType</span>(<span class="pl-c1">JSON</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"title"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"My Title"</span>)).
<span class="pl-en">extract</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"_links.next.href"</span>);

<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s1">nextTitleLink</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>You could also decide to instead return the entire response if you need to extract multiple values from the response:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Response response = 
given().
        param(&quot;param_name&quot;, &quot;param_value&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/title&quot;).
then().
        contentType(JSON).
        body(&quot;title&quot;, equalTo(&quot;My Title&quot;)).
extract().
        response(); 

String nextTitleLink = response.path(&quot;_links.next.href&quot;);
String headerValue = response.header(&quot;headerName&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">Response</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = 
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param_name"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"param_value"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/title"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">contentType</span>(<span class="pl-c1">JSON</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"title"</span>, <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"My Title"</span>)).
<span class="pl-en">extract</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">response</span>(); 

<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">nextTitleLink</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"_links.next.href"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">headerValue</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName"</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">JSON (using JsonPath)</h2><a id="user-content-json-using-jsonpath" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JSON (using JsonPath)" href="#json-using-jsonpath"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Once we have the response body we can then use the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/json-path/6.0.1/io/restassured/path/json/JsonPath.html" rel="nofollow">JsonPath</a> to get data from the response body:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="int lottoId = from(json).getInt(&quot;lotto.lottoId&quot;);
List&lt;Integer&gt; winnerIds = from(json).get(&quot;lotto.winners.winnerId&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">lottoId</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">json</span>).<span class="pl-en">getInt</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoId"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">Integer</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">winnerIds</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">json</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.winners.winnerId"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Or a bit more efficiently:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonPath jsonPath = new JsonPath(json).setRootPath(&quot;lotto&quot;);
int lottoId = jsonPath.getInt(&quot;lottoId&quot;);
List&lt;Integer&gt; winnerIds = jsonPath.get(&quot;winners.winnderId&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonPath</span> <span class="pl-s1">jsonPath</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonPath</span>(<span class="pl-s1">json</span>).<span class="pl-en">setRootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">lottoId</span> = <span class="pl-s1">jsonPath</span>.<span class="pl-en">getInt</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lottoId"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">Integer</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">winnerIds</span> = <span class="pl-s1">jsonPath</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"winners.winnderId"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Note that you can use <code>JsonPath</code> standalone without depending on REST Assured, see <a href="GettingStarted">getting started guide</a> for more info on this.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">JsonPath Configuration</h3><a id="user-content-jsonpath-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: JsonPath Configuration" href="#jsonpath-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can configure object de-serializers etc for JsonPath by configuring it, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonPath jsonPath = new JsonPath(SOME_JSON).using(new JsonPathConfig(&quot;UTF-8&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonPath</span> <span class="pl-s1">jsonPath</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonPath</span>(<span class="pl-c1">SOME_JSON</span>).<span class="pl-en">using</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonPathConfig</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-8"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to configure JsonPath statically so that all instances of JsonPath will shared the same configuration:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="JsonPath.config = new JsonPathConfig(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">JsonPath</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">JsonPathConfig</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-8"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>You can read more about JsonPath at <a href="http://www.jayway.com/2013/04/12/whats-new-in-rest-assured-1-8/" rel="nofollow">this blog</a>.</p>
<p>Note that the JsonPath implementation uses <a href="http://groovy-lang.org/processing-xml.html#_gpath" rel="nofollow">Groovy's GPath</a> syntax and is not to be confused with Jayway's <a href="https://github.com/jayway/JsonPath">JsonPath</a> implementation.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">XML (using XmlPath)</h2><a id="user-content-xml-using-xmlpath" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XML (using XmlPath)" href="#xml-using-xmlpath"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You also have the corresponding functionality for XML using  <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.restassured/xml-path/6.0.1/io/restassured/path/xml/XmlPath.html" rel="nofollow">XmlPath</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String xml = post(&quot;/greetXML?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe&quot;).andReturn().asString();
// Now use XmlPath to get the first and last name
String firstName = from(xml).get(&quot;greeting.firstName&quot;);
String lastName = from(xml).get(&quot;greeting.firstName&quot;);

// or a bit more efficiently:
XmlPath xmlPath = new XmlPath(xml).setRootPath(&quot;greeting&quot;);
String firstName = xmlPath.get(&quot;firstName&quot;);
String lastName = xmlPath.get(&quot;lastName&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">xml</span> = <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe"</span>).<span class="pl-en">andReturn</span>().<span class="pl-en">asString</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// Now use XmlPath to get the first and last name</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">firstName</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">xml</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.firstName"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">lastName</span> = <span class="pl-en">from</span>(<span class="pl-s1">xml</span>).<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting.firstName"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// or a bit more efficiently:</span>
<span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span> <span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span>(<span class="pl-s1">xml</span>).<span class="pl-en">setRootPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"greeting"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">firstName</span> = <span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>);
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">lastName</span> = <span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span>.<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Note that you can use <code>XmlPath</code> standalone without depending on REST Assured, see <a href="GettingStarted">getting started guide</a> for more info on this.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">XmlPath Configuration</h3><a id="user-content-xmlpath-configuration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: XmlPath Configuration" href="#xmlpath-configuration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can configure object de-serializers and charset for XmlPath by configuring it, for example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="XmlPath xmlPath = new XmlPath(SOME_XML).using(new XmlPathConfig(&quot;UTF-8&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span> <span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span>(<span class="pl-c1">SOME_XML</span>).<span class="pl-en">using</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">XmlPathConfig</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-8"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to configure XmlPath statically so that all instances of XmlPath will shared the same configuration:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="XmlPath.config = new XmlPathConfig(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span>.<span class="pl-s1">config</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">XmlPathConfig</span>(<span class="pl-s">"UTF-8"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>You can read more about XmlPath at <a href="http://www.jayway.com/2013/04/12/whats-new-in-rest-assured-1-8/" rel="nofollow">this blog</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Parsing HTML with XmlPath</h3><a id="user-content-parsing-html-with-xmlpath" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Parsing HTML with XmlPath" href="#parsing-html-with-xmlpath"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>By configuring XmlPath with <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/xml-path/6.0.1/io/restassured/path/xml/XmlPath.CompatibilityMode.html" rel="nofollow">compatibility mode</a> <code>HTML</code> you can also use the XmlPath syntax (Gpath) to parse HTML pages. For example if you want to extract the title of this HTML document:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html&gt;
&lt;head&gt;
    &lt;title&gt;my title&lt;/title&gt;
  &lt;/head&gt;
  &lt;body&gt;
    &lt;p&gt;paragraph 1&lt;/p&gt;
     &lt;br&gt;
    &lt;p&gt;paragraph 2&lt;/p&gt;
  &lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>my title<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>paragraph 1<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
     <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">br</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>paragraph 2<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">p</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p>you can configure XmlPath like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String html = ...
XmlPath xmlPath = new XmlPath(CompatibilityMode.HTML, html);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">html</span> = ...
<span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span> <span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">XmlPath</span>(<span class="pl-smi">CompatibilityMode</span>.<span class="pl-c1">HTML</span>, <span class="pl-s1">html</span>);</pre></div>
<p>and then extract the title like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="xmlPath.getString(&quot;html.head.title&quot;); // will return &quot;mytitle&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-s1">xmlPath</span>.<span class="pl-en">getString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"html.head.title"</span>); <span class="pl-c">// will return "mytitle"</span></pre></div>
<p>In this example we've statically imported: <code>io.restassured.path.xml.XmlPath.CompatibilityMode.HTML</code>;</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Single path</h2><a id="user-content-single-path" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Single path" href="#single-path"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If you only want to make a request and return a single path you can use a shortcut:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="int lottoId = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).path(&quot;lotto.lottoid&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">lottoId</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lotto.lottoid"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will automatically determine whether to use JsonPath or XmlPath based on the content-type of the response. If no content-type is defined then REST Assured will try to look at the <a href="#default-parser">default parser</a> if defined. You can also manually decide which path instance to use, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="String firstName = post(&quot;/greetXML?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe&quot;).andReturn().xmlPath().getString(&quot;firstName&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">firstName</span> = <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/greetXML?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe"</span>).<span class="pl-en">andReturn</span>().<span class="pl-en">xmlPath</span>().<span class="pl-en">getString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Options are <code>xmlPath</code>, <code>jsonPath</code> and <code>htmlPath</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Headers, cookies, status etc</h2><a id="user-content-headers-cookies-status-etc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Headers, cookies, status etc" href="#headers-cookies-status-etc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also get headers, cookies, status line and status code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Response response = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;);

// Get all headers
Headers allHeaders = response.getHeaders();
// Get a single header value:
String headerName = response.getHeader(&quot;headerName&quot;);

// Get all cookies as simple name-value pairs
Map&lt;String, String&gt; allCookies = response.getCookies();
// Get a single cookie value:
String cookieValue = response.getCookie(&quot;cookieName&quot;);

// Get status line
String statusLine = response.getStatusLine();
// Get status code
int statusCode = response.getStatusCode();"><pre><span class="pl-smi">Response</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// Get all headers</span>
<span class="pl-smi">Headers</span> <span class="pl-s1">allHeaders</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getHeaders</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// Get a single header value:</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">headerName</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getHeader</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// Get all cookies as simple name-value pairs</span>
<span class="pl-smi">Map</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>, <span class="pl-smi">String</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">allCookies</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getCookies</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// Get a single cookie value:</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">cookieValue</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getCookie</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cookieName"</span>);

<span class="pl-c">// Get status line</span>
<span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">statusLine</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getStatusLine</span>();
<span class="pl-c">// Get status code</span>
<span class="pl-smi">int</span> <span class="pl-s1">statusCode</span> = <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">getStatusCode</span>();</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Multi-value headers and cookies</h2><a id="user-content-multi-value-headers-and-cookies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Multi-value headers and cookies" href="#multi-value-headers-and-cookies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>A header and a cookie can contain several values for the same name.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Multi-value headers</h3><a id="user-content-multi-value-headers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Multi-value headers" href="#multi-value-headers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>To get all values for a header you need to first get the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Headers.html" rel="nofollow">Headers</a> object from the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/response/Response.html" rel="nofollow">Response</a> object. From the <code>Headers</code> instance you can get all values using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Headers.html#getValues(java.lang.String)" rel="nofollow">Headers.getValues(</a></p>) method which returns a <code>List</code> with all header values.
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Multi-value cookies</h3><a id="user-content-multi-value-cookies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Multi-value cookies" href="#multi-value-cookies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>To get all values for a cookie you need to first get the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Cookies.html" rel="nofollow">Cookies</a> object from the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/response/Response.html" rel="nofollow">Response</a> object. From the <code>Cookies</code> instance you can get all values using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Cookies.html#getValues(java.lang.String)" rel="nofollow">Cookies.getValues()</a> method which returns a <code>List</code> with all cookie values.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Detailed Cookies</h2><a id="user-content-detailed-cookies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Detailed Cookies" href="#detailed-cookies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If you need to get e.g. the comment, path or expiry date etc from a cookie you need get a <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Cookie.html" rel="nofollow">detailed cookie</a> from REST Assured. To do this you can use the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/response/ResponseOptions.html#getDetailedCookie-java.lang.String-" rel="nofollow">Response.getDetailedCookie(java.lang.String)</a> method. The detailed cookie then contains all attributes from the cookie.</p>
<p>You can also get all detailed response <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/http/Cookies.html" rel="nofollow">cookies</a> using the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/response/ResponseOptions.html#getDetailedCookies--" rel="nofollow">Response.getDetailedCookies()</a> method.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Specifying Request Data</h1><a id="user-content-specifying-request-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Specifying Request Data" href="#specifying-request-data"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Besides specifying request parameters you can also specify headers, cookies, body and content type.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Invoking HTTP resources</h2><a id="user-content-invoking-http-resources" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Invoking HTTP resources" href="#invoking-http-resources"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You typically perform a request by calling any of the "HTTP methods" in the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/specification/RequestSpecification.html" rel="nofollow">request specification</a>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().get(&quot;/x&quot;). ..;"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>). ..;</pre></div>
<p>Where <code>get</code> is the HTTP request method.</p>
<p>As of REST Assured 3.0.0 you can use any HTTP verb with your request by making use of the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/specification/RequestSpecification.html#request-java.lang.String-java.lang.String-" rel="nofollow">request</a> method.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
       request(&quot;CONNECT&quot;, &quot;/somewhere&quot;).
then().
       statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">request</span>(<span class="pl-s">"CONNECT"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"/somewhere"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>This will send a "connect" request to the server.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Parameters</h2><a id="user-content-parameters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Parameters" href="#parameters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Normally you specify parameters like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
       param(&quot;param1&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;).
       param(&quot;param2&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;).
when().
       get(&quot;/something&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>).
       <span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"param2"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will automatically try to determine which parameter type (i.e. query or form parameter) based on the HTTP method. In case of GET query parameters will automatically be used and in case of POST form parameters will be used. In some cases it's however important to separate between form and query parameters in a PUT or POST. You can then do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
       formParam(&quot;formParamName&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;).
       queryParam(&quot;queryParamName&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;).
when().
       post(&quot;/something&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"formParamName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>).
       <span class="pl-en">queryParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"queryParamName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
       <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Parameters can also be set directly on the url:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="..when().get(&quot;/name?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe&quot;);"><pre>..when().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/name?firstName=John&amp;lastName=Doe"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>For multi-part parameters please refer to the <a href="#multi-part-form-data">Multi-part form data</a> section.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Multi-value parameter</h3><a id="user-content-multi-value-parameter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Multi-value parameter" href="#multi-value-parameter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Multi-value parameters are parameters with more then one value per parameter name (i.e. a list of values per name). You can specify these either by using var-args:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().param(&quot;myList&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"myList"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>). .. </pre></div>
<p>or using a list:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="List&lt;String&gt; values = new ArrayList&lt;String&gt;();
values.add(&quot;value1&quot;);
values.add(&quot;value2&quot;);

given().param(&quot;myList&quot;, values). .. "><pre><span class="pl-smi">List</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>&gt; <span class="pl-s1">values</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">ArrayList</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">String</span>&gt;();
<span class="pl-s1">values</span>.<span class="pl-en">add</span>(<span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>);
<span class="pl-s1">values</span>.<span class="pl-en">add</span>(<span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>);

<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"myList"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">values</span>). .. </pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">No-value parameter</h3><a id="user-content-no-value-parameter" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: No-value parameter" href="#no-value-parameter"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also specify a query, request or form parameter without a value at all:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().param(&quot;paramName&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">param</span>(<span class="pl-s">"paramName"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Path parameters</h3><a id="user-content-path-parameters" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Path parameters" href="#path-parameters"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also specify so called path parameters in your request, e.g.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="post(&quot;/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}&quot;, &quot;My Hotel&quot;, 23);"><pre><span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"My Hotel"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">23</span>);</pre></div>
<p>These kinds of path parameters are referred to "unnamed path parameters" in REST Assured since they are index based (<code>hotelId</code> will be equal to "My Hotel" since it's the first placeholder).</p>
<p>You can also use named path parameters:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        pathParam(&quot;hotelId&quot;, &quot;My Hotel&quot;).
        pathParam(&quot;roomNumber&quot;, 23).
when(). 
        post(&quot;/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}&quot;).
then().
         .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">pathParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"hotelId"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"My Hotel"</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">pathParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"roomNumber"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">23</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>(). 
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         ..</pre></div>
<p>Path parameters makes it easier to read the request path as well as enabling the request path to easily be re-usable in many tests with different parameter values.</p>
<p>As of version 2.8.0 you can mix unnamed and named path parameters:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        pathParam(&quot;hotelId&quot;, &quot;My Hotel&quot;).        
when(). 
        post(&quot;/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}&quot;, 23).
then().
         .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">pathParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"hotelId"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"My Hotel"</span>).        
<span class="pl-en">when</span>(). 
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/reserve/{hotelId}/{roomNumber}"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">23</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
         ..</pre></div>
<p>Here <code>roomNumber</code> will be replaced with <code>23</code>.</p>
<p>Note that specifying too few or too many parameters will result in an error message. For advanced use cases you can add, change, remove (even redundant path parameters) from a <a href="#filters">filter</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Cookies</h2><a id="user-content-cookies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cookies" href="#cookies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In its simplest form you specify cookies like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().cookie(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;John&quot;).when().get(&quot;/cookie&quot;).then().body(equalTo(&quot;username&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookie</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/cookie"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>You can also specify a multi-value cookie like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().cookie(&quot;cookieName&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookie</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cookieName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>This will create <em>two</em> cookies, <code>cookieName=value1</code> and <code>cookieName=value2</code>.</p>
<p>You can also specify a detailed cookie using:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Cookie someCookie = new Cookie.Builder(&quot;some_cookie&quot;, &quot;some_value&quot;).setSecured(true).setComment(&quot;some comment&quot;).build();
given().cookie(someCookie).when().get(&quot;/cookie&quot;).then().assertThat().body(equalTo(&quot;x&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span> <span class="pl-s1">someCookie</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span>.<span class="pl-smi">Builder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some_cookie"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"some_value"</span>).<span class="pl-en">setSecured</span>(<span class="pl-c1">true</span>).<span class="pl-en">setComment</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some comment"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookie</span>(<span class="pl-s1">someCookie</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/cookie"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>or several detailed cookies at the same time:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Cookie cookie1 = Cookie.Builder(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;John&quot;).setComment(&quot;comment 1&quot;).build();
Cookie cookie2 = Cookie.Builder(&quot;token&quot;, 1234).setComment(&quot;comment 2&quot;).build();
Cookies cookies = new Cookies(cookie1, cookie2);
given().cookies(cookies).when().get(&quot;/cookie&quot;).then().body(equalTo(&quot;username, token&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span> <span class="pl-s1">cookie1</span> = <span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span>.<span class="pl-en">Builder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>).<span class="pl-en">setComment</span>(<span class="pl-s">"comment 1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();
<span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span> <span class="pl-s1">cookie2</span> = <span class="pl-smi">Cookie</span>.<span class="pl-en">Builder</span>(<span class="pl-s">"token"</span>, <span class="pl-c1">1234</span>).<span class="pl-en">setComment</span>(<span class="pl-s">"comment 2"</span>).<span class="pl-en">build</span>();
<span class="pl-smi">Cookies</span> <span class="pl-s1">cookies</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">Cookies</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cookie1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">cookie2</span>);
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookies</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cookies</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/cookie"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username, token"</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Headers</h2><a id="user-content-headers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Headers" href="#headers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().header(&quot;MyHeader&quot;, &quot;Something&quot;).and(). ..
given().headers(&quot;MyHeader&quot;, &quot;Something&quot;, &quot;MyOtherHeader&quot;, &quot;SomethingElse&quot;).and(). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"MyHeader"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Something"</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>(). ..
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">headers</span>(<span class="pl-s">"MyHeader"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Something"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"MyOtherHeader"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"SomethingElse"</span>).<span class="pl-en">and</span>(). ..</pre></div>
<p>You can also specify a multi-value headers like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().header(&quot;headerName&quot;, &quot;value1&quot;, &quot;value2&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"value2"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>This will create <em>two</em> headers, <code>headerName: value1</code> and <code>headerName: value2</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">Header Merging/Overwriting</h4><a id="user-content-header-mergingoverwriting" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Header Merging/Overwriting" href="#header-mergingoverwriting"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>By default headers are merged. So for example if you do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().header(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;1&quot;).header(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;2&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"1"</span>).<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"2"</span>). ..</pre></div>
