<p>The request will contain two headers, "x: 1" and "x: 2". You can change in this on a per header basis in the <a href="http://static.javadoc.io/io.rest-assured/rest-assured/6.0.1/io/restassured/config/HeaderConfig.html" rel="nofollow">HeaderConfig</a>. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssuredConfig.config().headerConfig(headerConfig().overwriteHeadersWithName(&quot;x&quot;))).
        header(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;1&quot;).
        header(&quot;x&quot;, &quot;2&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/something&quot;).
..."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssuredConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">headerConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">headerConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">overwriteHeadersWithName</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>))).
        <span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"1"</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"x"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"2"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).
...</pre></div>
<p>This means that only one header, "x: 2", is sent to server.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Content Type</h2><a id="user-content-content-type" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Content Type" href="#content-type"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().contentType(ContentType.TEXT). ..
given().contentType(&quot;application/json&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">contentType</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ContentType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">TEXT</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">contentType</span>(<span class="pl-s">"application/json"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>You can also instruct Rest Assured to don't include any (default) content-type at all:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().noContentType(). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">noContentType</span>(). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Request Body</h2><a id="user-content-request-body" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Request Body" href="#request-body"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().body(&quot;some body&quot;). .. // Works for POST, PUT and DELETE requests
given().request().body(&quot;some body&quot;). .. // More explicit (optional)"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some body"</span>). .. <span class="pl-c">// Works for POST, PUT and DELETE requests</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">request</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"some body"</span>). .. <span class="pl-c">// More explicit (optional)</span></pre></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().body(new byte[]{42}). .. // Works for POST, PUT and DELETE
given().request().body(new byte[]{42}). .. // More explicit (optional)"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">byte</span>[]{<span class="pl-c1">42</span>}). .. <span class="pl-c">// Works for POST, PUT and DELETE</span>
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">request</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">byte</span>[]{<span class="pl-c1">42</span>}). .. <span class="pl-c">// More explicit (optional)</span></pre></div>
<p>You can also serialize a Java object to JSON or XML. Click <a href="#serialization">here</a> for details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Verifying Response Data</h1><a id="user-content-verifying-response-data" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Verifying Response Data" href="#verifying-response-data"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can also verify status code, status line, cookies, headers, content type and body.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Response Body</h2><a id="user-content-response-body" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Response Body" href="#response-body"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>See Usage examples, e.g. <a href="#example-1---json">JSON</a> or <a href="#example-2---xml">XML</a>.</p>
<p>You can also map a response body to a Java Object, click <a href="#deserialization">here</a> for details.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Cookies</h2><a id="user-content-cookies-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Cookies" href="#cookies-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().cookie(&quot;cookieName&quot;, &quot;cookieValue&quot;). ..
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().cookies(&quot;cookieName1&quot;, &quot;cookieValue1&quot;, &quot;cookieName2&quot;, &quot;cookieValue2&quot;). ..
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().cookies(&quot;cookieName1&quot;, &quot;cookieValue1&quot;, &quot;cookieName2&quot;, containsString(&quot;Value2&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookie</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cookieName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieValue"</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookies</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cookieName1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieValue1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieName2"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieValue2"</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">cookies</span>(<span class="pl-s">"cookieName1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieValue1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"cookieName2"</span>, <span class="pl-en">containsString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Value2"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Status</h2><a id="user-content-status" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Status" href="#status"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().statusCode(200). ..

get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().statusLine(containsString(&quot;some&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusLine</span>(<span class="pl-s">"something"</span>). ..

<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Headers</h2><a id="user-content-headers-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Headers" href="#headers-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().header(&quot;headerName&quot;, &quot;headerValue&quot;). ..
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().headers(&quot;headerName1&quot;, &quot;headerValue1&quot;, &quot;headerName2&quot;, &quot;headerValue2&quot;). ..
get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().headers(&quot;headerName1&quot;, &quot;headerValue1&quot;, &quot;headerName2&quot;, containsString(&quot;Value2&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerValue"</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">headers</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerValue1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerName2"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerValue2"</span>). ..
<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">headers</span>(<span class="pl-s">"headerName1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerValue1"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"headerName2"</span>, <span class="pl-en">containsString</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Value2"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<p>It's also possible to use a mapping function when validating headers. For example let's say you want to validate that the <code>Content-Length</code> header is less than 1000. You can then use a mapping function to first convert the header value to an int and then use an <code>Integer</code> before validating it with a Hamcrest matcher:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/something&quot;).then().assertThat().header(&quot;Content-Length&quot;, Integer::parseInt, lessThan(1000));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/something"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"Content-Length"</span>, <span class="pl-smi">Integer</span>::<span class="pl-s1">parseInt</span>, <span class="pl-en">lessThan</span>(<span class="pl-c1">1000</span>));</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Content-Type</h2><a id="user-content-content-type-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Content-Type" href="#content-type-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().contentType(ContentType.JSON). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">contentType</span>(<span class="pl-smi">ContentType</span>.<span class="pl-c1">JSON</span>). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Full body/content matching</h2><a id="user-content-full-bodycontent-matching" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Full body/content matching" href="#full-bodycontent-matching"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().assertThat().body(equalTo(&quot;something&quot;)). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">assertThat</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"something"</span>)). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Use the response to verify other parts of the response</h2><a id="user-content-use-the-response-to-verify-other-parts-of-the-response" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Use the response to verify other parts of the response" href="#use-the-response-to-verify-other-parts-of-the-response"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>You can use data from the response to verify another part of the response. For example consider the following JSON document returned from service x:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-js notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{ &quot;userId&quot; : &quot;some-id&quot;, &quot;href&quot; : &quot;http://localhost:8080/some-id&quot; }"><pre><span class="pl-kos">{</span> <span class="pl-s">"userId"</span> : <span class="pl-s">"some-id"</span><span class="pl-kos">,</span> <span class="pl-s">"href"</span> : <span class="pl-s">"http://localhost:8080/some-id"</span> <span class="pl-kos">}</span></pre></div>
<p>You may notice that the "href" attribute ends with the value of the "userId" attribute. If we want to verify this we can implement a <code>io.restassured.matcher.ResponseAwareMatcher</code> and use it like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().body(&quot;href&quot;, new ResponseAwareMatcher&lt;Response&gt;() {
                                  public Matcher&lt;?&gt; matcher(Response response) {
                                          return equalTo(&quot;http://localhost:8080/&quot; + response.path(&quot;userId&quot;));
                                  }
                       });"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"href"</span>, <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">ResponseAwareMatcher</span>&lt;<span class="pl-smi">Response</span>&gt;() {
                                  <span class="pl-k">public</span> <span class="pl-smi">Matcher</span>&lt;?&gt; <span class="pl-en">matcher</span>(<span class="pl-smi">Response</span> <span class="pl-s1">response</span>) {
                                          <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"http://localhost:8080/"</span> + <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"userId"</span>));
                                  }
                       });</pre></div>
<p>If you're using Java 8 you can use a lambda expression instead:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().body(&quot;href&quot;, response -&gt; equalTo(&quot;http://localhost:8080/&quot; + response.path(&quot;userId&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"href"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">response</span> -&gt; <span class="pl-en">equalTo</span>(<span class="pl-s">"http://localhost:8080/"</span> + <span class="pl-s1">response</span>.<span class="pl-en">path</span>(<span class="pl-s">"userId"</span>));</pre></div>
<p>There are some predefined matchers that you can use defined in the <code>io.restassured.matcher.RestAssuredMatchers</code> (or <code>io.restassured.module.mockmvc.matcher.RestAssuredMockMvcMatchers</code> if using the spring-mock-mvc module). For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().body(&quot;href&quot;, endsWithPath(&quot;userId&quot;));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"href"</span>, <span class="pl-en">endsWithPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"userId"</span>));</pre></div>
<p><code>ResponseAwareMatchers</code> can also be composed, either with another <code>ResponseAwareMatcher</code> or with a Hamcrest Matcher. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="get(&quot;/x&quot;).then().body(&quot;href&quot;, and(startsWith(&quot;http:/localhost:8080/&quot;), endsWithPath(&quot;userId&quot;)));"><pre><span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/x"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">body</span>(<span class="pl-s">"href"</span>, <span class="pl-en">and</span>(<span class="pl-en">startsWith</span>(<span class="pl-s">"http:/localhost:8080/"</span>), <span class="pl-en">endsWithPath</span>(<span class="pl-s">"userId"</span>)));</pre></div>
<p>The <code>and</code> method is statically imported from <code>io.restassured.matcher.ResponseAwareMatcherComposer</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Measuring Response Time</h2><a id="user-content-measuring-response-time" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Measuring Response Time" href="#measuring-response-time"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of version 2.8.0 REST Assured has support measuring response time. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="long timeInMs = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).time()"><pre><span class="pl-smi">long</span> <span class="pl-s1">timeInMs</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">time</span>()</pre></div>
<p>or using a specific time unit:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="long timeInSeconds = get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).timeIn(SECONDS);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">long</span> <span class="pl-s1">timeInSeconds</span> = <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).<span class="pl-en">timeIn</span>(<span class="pl-c1">SECONDS</span>);</pre></div>
<p>where <code>SECONDS</code> is just a standard <code>TimeUnit</code>. You can also validate it using the validation DSL:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
      get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).
then().
      time(lessThan(2000L)); // Milliseconds"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">time</span>(<span class="pl-en">lessThan</span>(<span class="pl-c1">2000L</span>)); <span class="pl-c">// Milliseconds</span></pre></div>
<p>or</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="when().
      get(&quot;/lotto&quot;).
then().
      time(lessThan(2L), SECONDS);"><pre><span class="pl-en">when</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/lotto"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
      <span class="pl-en">time</span>(<span class="pl-en">lessThan</span>(<span class="pl-c1">2L</span>), <span class="pl-c1">SECONDS</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Please note that response time measurement should be performed when the JVM is hot! (i.e. running a response time measurement when only running a single test will yield erroneous results). Also note that you can only vaguely regard these measurments to correlate with the server request processing time (since the response time will include the HTTP round trip and REST Assured processing time among other things).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h1 class="heading-element">Authentication</h1><a id="user-content-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Authentication" href="#authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>REST assured also supports several authentication schemes, for example OAuth, digest, certificate, form and preemptive basic authentication. You can either set authentication for each request:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().basic(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">basic</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>but you can also define authentication for all requests:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="RestAssured.authentication = basic(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">RestAssured</span>.<span class="pl-s1">authentication</span> = <span class="pl-en">basic</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>);</pre></div>
<p>or you can use a <a href="#specification-re-use">specification</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Basic Authentication</h2><a id="user-content-basic-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Basic Authentication" href="#basic-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>There are two types of basic authentication, preemptive and "challenged basic authentication".</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Preemptive Basic Authentication</h3><a id="user-content-preemptive-basic-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Preemptive Basic Authentication" href="#preemptive-basic-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>This will send the basic authentication credential even before the server gives an unauthorized response in certain situations, thus reducing the overhead of making an additional connection. This is typically what you want to use in most situations unless you're testing the servers ability to challenge. Example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().preemptive().basic(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;).when().get(&quot;/secured/hello&quot;).then().statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">preemptive</span>().<span class="pl-en">basic</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/secured/hello"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">Challenged Basic Authentication</h3><a id="user-content-challenged-basic-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Challenged Basic Authentication" href="#challenged-basic-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>When using "challenged basic authentication" REST Assured will not supply the credentials unless the server has explicitly asked for it. This means that REST Assured will make an additional request to the server in order to be challenged and then follow up with the same request once more but this time setting the basic credentials in the header.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().basic(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;).when().get(&quot;/secured/hello&quot;).then().statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">basic</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/secured/hello"</span>).<span class="pl-en">then</span>().<span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Digest Authentication</h2><a id="user-content-digest-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Digest Authentication" href="#digest-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Currently only "challenged digest authentication" is supported. Example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().digest(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;).when().get(&quot;/secured&quot;). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">digest</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>).<span class="pl-en">when</span>().<span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/secured"</span>). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Form Authentication</h2><a id="user-content-form-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Form Authentication" href="#form-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Form-based_authentication" rel="nofollow">Form authentication</a> is very popular on the internet. It's typically associated with a user filling out his credentials (username and password) on a webpage and then pressing a login button of some sort. A very simple HTML page that provide the basis for form authentication may look like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html&gt;
  &lt;head&gt;
    &lt;title&gt;Login&lt;/title&gt;
  &lt;/head&gt;

  &lt;body&gt;
    &lt;form action=&quot;j_spring_security_check&quot; method=&quot;POST&quot;&gt;
      &lt;table&gt;
        &lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;User:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;input type='text' name='j_username'&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
        &lt;tr&gt;&lt;td&gt;Password:&lt;/td&gt;&lt;td&gt;&lt;input type='password' name='j_password'&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
          &lt;tr&gt;&lt;td colspan='2'&gt;&lt;input name=&quot;submit&quot; type=&quot;submit&quot;/&gt;&lt;/td&gt;&lt;/tr&gt;
       &lt;/table&gt;
        &lt;/form&gt;
      &lt;/body&gt;
 &lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Login<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>

  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
    <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">form</span> <span class="pl-c1">action</span>="<span class="pl-s">j_spring_security_check</span>" <span class="pl-c1">method</span>="<span class="pl-s">POST</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">table</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>User:&amp;nbsp;<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>='<span class="pl-s">text</span>' <span class="pl-c1">name</span>='<span class="pl-s">j_username</span>'<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Password:<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>='<span class="pl-s">password</span>' <span class="pl-c1">name</span>='<span class="pl-s">j_password</span>'<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
          <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span> <span class="pl-c1">colspan</span>='<span class="pl-s">2</span>'<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">submit</span>" <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">submit</span>"<span class="pl-kos">/&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
       <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">table</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
        <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">form</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
 <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p>I.e. the server expects the user to fill-out the "j_username" and "j_password" input fields and then press "submit" to login. With REST Assured you can test a service protected by form authentication like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        auth().form(&quot;John&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;).
when().
        get(&quot;/formAuth&quot;);
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">form</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/formAuth"</span>);
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>While this may work it's not optimal. What happens when form authentication is used like this in REST Assured an additional request have to made to the server in order to retrieve the webpage with the login details. REST Assured will then try to parse this page and look for two input fields (with username and password) as well as the form action URI. This may work or fail depending on the complexity of the webpage. A better option is to supply the these details when setting up the form authentication. In this case one could do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        auth().form(&quot;John&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;, new FormAuthConfig(&quot;/j_spring_security_check&quot;, &quot;j_username&quot;, &quot;j_password&quot;)).
when().
        get(&quot;/formAuth&quot;);
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">form</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>, <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">FormAuthConfig</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/j_spring_security_check"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"j_username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"j_password"</span>)).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/formAuth"</span>);
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>This way REST Assured doesn't need to make an additional request and parse the webpage. There's also a predefined FormAuthConfig called <code>springSecurity</code> that you can use if you're using the default Spring Security properties:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        auth().form(&quot;John&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;, FormAuthConfig.springSecurity()).
when().
        get(&quot;/formAuth&quot;);
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">form</span>(<span class="pl-s">"John"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>, <span class="pl-smi">FormAuthConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">springSecurity</span>()).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/formAuth"</span>);
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">CSRF</h3><a id="user-content-csrf" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF" href="#csrf"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Today it's common for the server to supply a <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cross-site_request_forgery" rel="nofollow">CSRF</a> token with the response in order to avoid these kinds of attacks. REST Assured has support for automatically parsing and supplying the CSRF token to the server. In order for this to work REST Assured <em>must</em> make an additional request and parse (parts) of the website.</p>
<p>REST Assured supports two ways of providing CSRF tokens to the server, either by submitting the CSRF token in a form, or as a header.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">CSRF Form Token</h4><a id="user-content-csrf-form-token" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF Form Token" href="#csrf-form-token"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Consider that we have a simple website that adds a user:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html&gt;
&lt;head&gt;
&lt;title&gt;Add User&lt;/title&gt;
&lt;/head&gt;
&lt;body&gt;
&lt;form action=&quot;/users&quot; method=&quot;POST&quot;&gt;
&lt;table&gt;
  &lt;tr&gt;
      &lt;td&gt;First Name:&amp;nbsp;&lt;/td&gt;
      &lt;td&gt;&lt;input type=&quot;text&quot; name=&quot;firstName&quot;&gt;&lt;/td&gt;
  &lt;/tr&gt;
  &lt;tr&gt;
      &lt;td&gt;Last Name:&lt;/td&gt;
      &lt;td&gt;&lt;input type=&quot;text&quot; name=&quot;lastName&quot;&gt;&lt;/td&gt;
  &lt;/tr&gt;
  &lt;tr&gt;
      &lt;td colspan=&quot;2&quot;&gt;&lt;input name=&quot;submit&quot; type=&quot;submit&quot;/&gt;&lt;/td&gt;
  &lt;/tr&gt;
&lt;/table&gt;
&lt;input type=&quot;hidden&quot; name=&quot;_csrf&quot; value=&quot;8adf2ea1-b246-40aa-8e13-a85fb7914341&quot;/&gt;
&lt;/form&gt;
&lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Add User<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">form</span> <span class="pl-c1">action</span>="<span class="pl-s">/users</span>" <span class="pl-c1">method</span>="<span class="pl-s">POST</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">table</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>First Name:&amp;nbsp;<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">text</span>" <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">firstName</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Last Name:<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">text</span>" <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">lastName</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
      <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span> <span class="pl-c1">colspan</span>="<span class="pl-s">2</span>"<span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">submit</span>" <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">submit</span>"<span class="pl-kos">/&gt;</span><span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">td</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">tr</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">table</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">input</span> <span class="pl-c1">type</span>="<span class="pl-s">hidden</span>" <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">_csrf</span>" <span class="pl-c1">value</span>="<span class="pl-s">8adf2ea1-b246-40aa-8e13-a85fb7914341</span>"<span class="pl-kos">/&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">form</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p>We can see that the form contains a <code>hidden</code> input field with a CSRF token. If we want to test posting a new user to <code>/users</code> we need to include this CSRF token in the request.
We can do this by doing:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        csrf(&quot;/users&quot;, &quot;_csrf&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;)
when().
        post(&quot;/users&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"_csrf"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>)
when().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will now first perform a <code>GET</code> request to <code>/users</code> (the first argument to the <code>csrf</code> method) and it expects the form to have a (hidden) input field named <code>_csrf</code> (second argument to the <code>csrf</code> method). REST Assured will then find <code>8adf2ea1-b246-40aa-8e13-a85fb7914341</code> and include it as a form paramter in the <code>POST</code> request to <code>/users</code>.</p>
<p>Since <code>_csrf</code> is the default expect input field name for CSRF tokens in REST Assured, we could have skipped it and just do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        csrf(&quot;/users&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;)
when().
        post(&quot;/users&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>)
when().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>which would work as well. You can configure the default CSRF form token name in the config, e.g. to change the default name to <code>_mycsrf</code> you can do: <code>RestAssured.config = RestAssuredConfig.config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().with().csrfInputFieldName("_mycsrf"));</code></p>
<p>The config also supports, among other things, to set a default resource to get CSRF token from for all requests.</p>
<p>Note that it's common to get the CSRF token from one resource but <code>POST</code> to another resource. Let's say that, in the example above, the "add user" form is located at <code>/users/form</code>, you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        csrf(&quot;/users/form&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;)
when().
        post(&quot;/users&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users/form"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>)
when().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">CSRF Header Token</h4><a id="user-content-csrf-header-token" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF Header Token" href="#csrf-header-token"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Besides sending CSRF tokens in forms, REST Assured also support sending a CSRF token in a header. For example, if you have a login page at <code>/login</code> that looks like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-html-basic notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;html&gt;
&lt;head&gt;
  &lt;title&gt;Login&lt;/title&gt;
  &lt;meta name=&quot;_csrf_header&quot; content=&quot;ab8722b1-1f23-4dcf-bf63-fb8b94be4107&quot;/&gt;
&lt;/head&gt;
&lt;body&gt;
   ..
&lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;"><pre><span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>Login<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">title</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
  <span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">meta</span> <span class="pl-c1">name</span>="<span class="pl-s">_csrf_header</span>" <span class="pl-c1">content</span>="<span class="pl-s">ab8722b1-1f23-4dcf-bf63-fb8b94be4107</span>"<span class="pl-kos">/&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">head</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
   ..
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">body</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">&lt;/</span><span class="pl-ent">html</span><span class="pl-kos">&gt;</span></pre></div>
<p>The csrf meta tag name is called <code>_csrf_header</code> (which is the default meta tag name used by REST Assured). You can do like this to POST to <code>/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf</code> and make REST Assured include the CSRF token (<code>ab8722b1-1f23-4dcf-bf63-fb8b94be4107</code>) in a header called <code>X-CSRF-TOKEN</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
when().
        post(&quot;/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/login"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>You can configure a different the meta name (if it's not called <code>_csrf_header</code>) like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssuredConfig.config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().csrfMetaTagName(&quot;_my_csrf_header&quot;))).
        csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
when().
        post(&quot;/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssuredConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfMetaTagName</span>(<span class="pl-s">"_my_csrf_header"</span>))).
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/login"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Additionally, you can change the header name:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(RestAssuredConfig.config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().csrfHeaderName(&quot;MyHeader&quot;))).
        csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
when().
        post(&quot;/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre class="notranslate"><code>given().
        config(RestAssuredConfig.config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().csrfHeaderName("MyHeader"))).
        csrf("/login").
when().
        post("/pageThatRequireHeaderCsrf").
then().
        statusCode(200);
</code></pre></div>
<p>REST Assured will now send the CSRF token in a header called <code>MyHeader</code> instead of the default, <code>X-CSRF-TOKEN</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">CSRF and Authentication</h4><a id="user-content-csrf-and-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF and Authentication" href="#csrf-and-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>The page you GET to extract the CSRF token might be protected by authentication. REST Assured automatically applies authentication to the CSRF resource as well if defined in the DSL. For example, let's say that the <code>/users</code> (see above) resources requires basic authentication for both GET and POST. You can then specify authentication as you normally would and this would be applied to the CSRF request as well:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        auth().preemptive().basic(&quot;username&quot;, &quot;password&quot;)
        csrf(&quot;/users&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;firstName&quot;, &quot;John&quot;)
        formParam(&quot;lastName&quot;, &quot;Doe&quot;)
when().
        post(&quot;/users&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">preemptive</span>().<span class="pl-en">basic</span>(<span class="pl-s">"username"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"password"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"firstName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"John"</span>)
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"lastName"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"Doe"</span>)
when().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">CSRF Prioritization</h4><a id="user-content-csrf-prioritization" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF Prioritization" href="#csrf-prioritization"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>If the page you use to get the CSRF token both contains a header token <em>and</em> a (different) CSRF form token you can instruct REST Assured which one to prioritize:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().config(RestAssuredConfig.config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().csrfPrioritization(CsrfPrioritization.HEADER))). .. "><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">config</span>(<span class="pl-smi">RestAssuredConfig</span>.<span class="pl-en">config</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>(<span class="pl-en">csrfConfig</span>().<span class="pl-en">csrfPrioritization</span>(<span class="pl-smi">CsrfPrioritization</span>.<span class="pl-c1">HEADER</span>))). .. </pre></div>
<p>Change <code>CsrfPrioritization.HEADER</code> to <code>CsrfPrioritization.FORM</code> to prioritize form tokens instead.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h4 class="heading-element">CSRF Cookie Propagation</h4><a id="user-content-csrf-cookie-propagation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: CSRF Cookie Propagation" href="#csrf-cookie-propagation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>As of version 5.5.3, REST Assured automatically forwards the cookies from the GET request that returns the CSRF token and applies them to the "actual" request. These cookies will also be applied to the <code>CookieFilter</code> automatically (if configured) and <a href="#session-filter">SessionFilter</a> (if configured). For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
    csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
    formParam(&quot;name&quot;, &quot;My New Name&quot;).
when().
    post(&quot;/users/123&quot;).
then().
    statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/login"</span>).
    <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"name"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"My New Name"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users/123"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
    <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>Now the cookies returned from the GET request to login will be automatically applied to the POST to "/users/123".</p>
<p>If you have a CookieFilter defined for multiple requests, the cookies returned by GET to /login will be automatically stored in the CookieFilter and used in the second request.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="var cookieFilter = new CookieFilter()
given().
        filter(cookieFilter).
        csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
        formParam(&quot;name&quot;, &quot;My New Name&quot;).
when().
        post(&quot;/users/123&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);

given().
        filter(cookieFilter).
when().
        get(&quot;/users/123&quot;).
then().
        statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-smi">var</span> <span class="pl-s1">cookieFilter</span> = <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-smi">CookieFilter</span>()
<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">filter</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cookieFilter</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">csrf</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/login"</span>).
        <span class="pl-en">formParam</span>(<span class="pl-s">"name"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"My New Name"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">post</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users/123"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);

<span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">filter</span>(<span class="pl-s1">cookieFilter</span>).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/users/123"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
<p>You can disable this behavior by setting automaticallyApplyCookies to false the csrf config:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        config(config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().automaticallyApplyCookies(false))).
        csrf(&quot;/login&quot;).
when().
        ..."><pre class="notranslate"><code>given().
        config(config().csrfConfig(csrfConfig().automaticallyApplyCookies(false))).
        csrf("/login").
when().
        ...
</code></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">OAuth</h2><a id="user-content-oauth" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OAuth" href="#oauth"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>In order to use OAuth 1 and OAuth 2 (for query parameter signing) you need to add <a href="https://github.com/fernandezpablo85/scribe-java">Scribe</a> to your classpath (if you're using version 2.1.0 or older of REST Assured then please refer to the <a href="Usage_Legacy#OAuth">legacy</a> documentation). In Maven you can simply add the following dependency:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-text-xml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;dependency&gt;
            &lt;groupId&gt;com.github.scribejava&lt;/groupId&gt;
            &lt;artifactId&gt;scribejava-apis&lt;/artifactId&gt;
            &lt;version&gt;2.5.3&lt;/version&gt;
            &lt;scope&gt;test&lt;/scope&gt;
&lt;/dependency&gt;"><pre>&lt;<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;
            &lt;<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;com.github.scribejava&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">groupId</span>&gt;
            &lt;<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;scribejava-apis&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">artifactId</span>&gt;
            &lt;<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;2.5.3&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">version</span>&gt;
            &lt;<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;test&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">scope</span>&gt;
&lt;/<span class="pl-ent">dependency</span>&gt;</pre></div>
<p>If you're not using Maven <a href="https://github.com/fernandezpablo85/scribe-java/releases">download</a> a Scribe release manually and put it in your classpath.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">OAuth 1</h3><a id="user-content-oauth-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OAuth 1" href="#oauth-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>OAuth 1 requires <a href="#oauth">Scribe</a> in the classpath. To use auth 1 authentication you can do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().oauth(..). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">oauth</span>(..). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h3 class="heading-element">OAuth 2</h3><a id="user-content-oauth-2" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: OAuth 2" href="#oauth-2"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Since version <code>2.5.0</code> you can use OAuth 2 authentication without depending on <a href="#oauth">Scribe</a>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().oauth2(accessToken). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">oauth2</span>(<span class="pl-s1">accessToken</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>This will put the OAuth2 <code>accessToken</code> in a header. To be more explicit you can also do:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().preemptive().oauth2(accessToken). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">preemptive</span>().<span class="pl-en">oauth2</span>(<span class="pl-s1">accessToken</span>). ..</pre></div>
<p>There reason why <code>given().auth().oauth2(..)</code> still exists is for backward compatibility (they do the same thing). If you need to provide the OAuth2 token in a query parameter you currently need <a href="#oauth">Scribe</a> in the classpath. Then you can do like this:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().auth().oauth2(accessToken, OAuthSignature.QUERY_STRING). .."><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().<span class="pl-en">auth</span>().<span class="pl-en">oauth2</span>(<span class="pl-s1">accessToken</span>, <span class="pl-smi">OAuthSignature</span>.<span class="pl-c1">QUERY_STRING</span>). ..</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading"><h2 class="heading-element">Custom Authentication</h2><a id="user-content-custom-authentication" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Custom Authentication" href="#custom-authentication"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p>Rest Assured allows you to create custom authentication providers. You do this by implementing the <code>io.restassured.spi.AuthFilter</code> interface (preferably) and apply it as a <a href="#filters">filter</a>. For example let's say that your security consists of adding together two headers together in a new header called "AUTH" (this is of course not secure). Then you can do that like this (Java 8 syntax):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-java notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="given().
        filter((requestSpec, responseSpec, ctx) -&gt; {
            String header1 = requestSpec.getHeaders().getValue(&quot;header1&quot;);
            String header2 = requestSpec.getHeaders().getValue(&quot;header2&quot;);
            requestSpec.header(&quot;AUTH&quot;, header1 + header2);
            return ctx.next(requestSpec, responseSpec);
        }).
when().
        get(&quot;/customAuth&quot;).
then().
  statusCode(200);"><pre><span class="pl-en">given</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">filter</span>((<span class="pl-s1">requestSpec</span>, <span class="pl-s1">responseSpec</span>, <span class="pl-s1">ctx</span>) -&gt; {
            <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">header1</span> = <span class="pl-s1">requestSpec</span>.<span class="pl-en">getHeaders</span>().<span class="pl-en">getValue</span>(<span class="pl-s">"header1"</span>);
            <span class="pl-smi">String</span> <span class="pl-s1">header2</span> = <span class="pl-s1">requestSpec</span>.<span class="pl-en">getHeaders</span>().<span class="pl-en">getValue</span>(<span class="pl-s">"header2"</span>);
            <span class="pl-s1">requestSpec</span>.<span class="pl-en">header</span>(<span class="pl-s">"AUTH"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">header1</span> + <span class="pl-s1">header2</span>);
            <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">ctx</span>.<span class="pl-en">next</span>(<span class="pl-s1">requestSpec</span>, <span class="pl-s1">responseSpec</span>);
        }).
<span class="pl-en">when</span>().
        <span class="pl-en">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/customAuth"</span>).
<span class="pl-en">then</span>().
  <span class="pl-en">statusCode</span>(<span class="pl-c1">200</span>);</pre></div>
